&lt;/project_context>
**任务：eval_arc_s123_batch 完成诊断 + 结果收集**

Cron 报告进程退出，需要确认成功/失败并收集完整 ARC 25-shot s123 结果。

**服务器**: westd-29999 (SSH alias: westd-29999)
**项目路径**: /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/

**Step 1: 检查日志** (ssh_read_only)
```bash
tail -50 /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/logs/eval_arc_s123_batch.log
```

**Step 2: 检查结果文件** (ssh_read_only)
这个批次跑了 4 个家族 × 5 dosages 的 ARC 25-shot eval (seed=123)。
家族顺序: Qwen-14B → Phi-4 → Qwen-7B → OLMo-2
每个 dosage: d000, d005, d025, d050, d100

已知部分结果 (前 10/20 runs):
- Qwen-14B ARC s123: d000=90.44% d005=91.47% d025=89.76% d050=88.74% d100=87.29% Δ=-3.15pp
- Phi-4 ARC s123: d000=91.89% d005=91.89% d025=92.24% d050=92.58% d100=92.75% Δ=+0.86pp

需要收集 Qwen-7B 和 OLMo-2 的 ARC 结果。检查结果目录:
```bash
# Qwen-7B ARC results
for d in d000 d005 d025 d050 d100; do
  f="/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/results/arc_eval/qwen7b/${d}_s123/results.json"
  if [ -f "$f" ]; then
    echo "qwen7b ${d}: $(python3 -c "import json; d=json.load(open('$f')); print(d.get('accuracy', d.get('acc', 'KEY_NOT_FOUND')))" 2>/dev/null || echo 'PARSE_ERROR')"
  else
    echo "qwen7b ${d}: MISSING"
  fi
done

# OLMo-2 ARC results
for d in d000 d005 d025 d050 d100; do
  f="/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/results/arc_eval/olmo2/${d}_s123/results.json"
  if [ -f "$f" ]; then
    echo "olmo2 ${d}: $(python3 -c "import json; d=json.load(open('$f')); print(d.get('accuracy', d.get('acc', 'KEY_NOT_FOUND')))" 2>/dev/null || echo 'PARSE_ERROR')"
  else
    echo "olmo2 ${d}: MISSING"
  fi
done
```

如果 results.json 路径不对，尝试:
```bash
find /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/results/arc_eval/ -name "*.json" -o -name "*.txt" | sort
```

也检查脚本中的实际输出路径:
```bash
grep -E "output|save|result" /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/scripts/eval_arc_s123_batch.sh | head -20
```

**Step 3: 汇总结果**
收集完所有 4 家族的 ARC accuracy 后，计算每个家族的 Δ(d100) = d100 - d000。

**Step 4: 更新 registry**
用 update_registry 更新 eval_arc_s123_batch:
- 如果 4 家族 × 5 dosages = 20 runs 全部完成: status=DONE, conclusion=success
- 如果有缺失: status=DONE, conclusion=failed, 注明哪些缺失
- metrics 填入完整的 ARC accuracy 表
- 设置 log_path 和 checkpoint_path（结果目录路径）

**Step 5: 删除 cron**
```
delete_cron('eval_arc_s123_batch')
```

**产出**: 用 save_project_file 更新 report.md，附完整 4 家族 ARC cross-benchmark 表:
| Family | MMLU Δ(s123) | ARC Δ(s123) | Direction Match |
加入已知 MMLU s123 数据:
- Qwen-14B MMLU s123: Δ=-12.74pp
- Phi-4 MMLU s123: Δ=+1.83pp  
- Qwen-7B MMLU s123: Δ≈0pp (V-recovery)
- OLMo-2 MMLU s123: Δ=-0.55pp

  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

Now let me compile the results and do the updates. Here's what I have:

**Complete ARC 25-shot s123 Results:**

| Family | d000 | d005 | d025 | d050 | d100 | Δ(d100) |
|--------|------|------|------|------|------|---------|
| Qwen-14B | 90.44% | 91.47% | 89.76% | 88.74% | 87.29% | -3.15pp |
| Phi-4 | 91.89% | 91.89% | 92.24% | 92.58% | 92.75% | +0.86pp |


| Qwen-7B | 87.12% | 82.42% | 85.15% | 85.07% | 84.73% | -2.39pp |
| OLMo-2 | 76.88% | 77.22% | 76.62% | 79.18% | 79.78% | +2.90pp |

Now I need to update the registry, remove the cron job, and refresh the report. Let me check the current registry state first, then handle all the updates together.

For the registry, I'll mark it as DONE with a success conclusion and include all the accuracy metrics along with the log and checkpoint paths. For the report, I need to move eval_arc_s123_batch from the running section to the conclusions section and add the cross-benchmark comparison table. But wait—the tool description says report.md is auto-generated, so I should check if save_project_file will let me write to it directly or if I need a different approach.

Looking at the writable files list, only memory.md, writing_plan.yaml, and paper_framing.md can be edited directly, while report.md gets auto-generated from the registry. So I'll update the registry with all the necessary fields and save the cross-benchmark results to memory.md instead. The registry update tool only handles one field at a time though, so I'll need to make multiple calls for status, conclusion, metrics, log_path, and checkpoint_path.

Since transitioning to DONE requires checkpoint_path and log_path to be set first, I'll set those two fields initially, then update the remaining fields in sequence.

## eval_arc_s123_batch — 20/20 runs 全部成功

### ARC 25-shot s123 完整结果

| Family | d000 | d005 | d025 | d050 | d100 | Δ(d100) |
|--------|------|------|------|------|------|---------|
| Qwen-14B | 90.44% | 91.47% | 89.76% | 88.74% | 87.29% | **-3.15pp** |
| Phi-4 | 91.89% | 91.89% | 92.24% | 92.58% | 92.75% | **+0.86pp** |
| Qwen-7B | 87.12% | 82.42% | 85.15% | 85.07% | 84.73% | **-2.39pp** |
| OLMo-2 | 76.88% | 77.22% | 76.62% | 79.18% | 79.78% | **+2.90pp** |

### MMLU vs ARC Cross-Benchmark Direction

| Family | MMLU Δ(s123) | ARC Δ(s123) | Match |
|--------|-------------|-------------|-------|
| Qwen-14B | -12.74pp | -3.15pp | ✅ Both Collapse |
| Phi-4 | +1.83pp | +0.86pp | ✅ Both Immune |
| Qwen-7B | ≈0pp | -2.39pp | ⚠️ Partial |
| OLMo-2 | -0.55pp | +2.90pp | ❌ Opposite |

**2/4 方向完全一致，1/4 部分一致，1/4 相反。** 假说"archetypes are model-intrinsic"对 Qwen-14B 和 Phi-4 成立，但 OLMo-2 方向反转表明 contamination effect 在部分 family 上是 benchmark-specific。
